<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sixteen shapes in the grid each contain a number with the following rules:

- The numbers in the circle are four more than the numbers in the squares on the same row.
- The numbers in the triangles are double those of the numbers in the squares on the same row.

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a sequence of numbers, each term after the first three terms is the sum of the previous three terms. The first three terms are $-3, 0, 2.$ Which is the first term to exceed $100?$

- $12^\text{th}$ term 
- $13^\text{th}$ term 
- $14^\text{th}$ term 
- $15^\text{th}$ term


</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I was exploring a puzzle in which headless match sticks had to be moved to make a different number of triangles. I made one small triangle

![papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i00.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i00.png "papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i00.png") 

$3$ matches

I made it into $4$ small triangles by adding $6$ matches.

![papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i01.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i01.png "papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-3/s01-q09-i01.png") 

$9$ matches

I added another row to make the next shape in the pattern and counted the number of small triangles and counted the matches. How many small triangles are in the next shape in the pattern?

How many matches are in the next shape in the pattern?


</div>

<emptyline>
</code></pre>

**Question**

Grange Hill School has the same number of pupils in each class, but there are more classes in year $7$ than in year $8$.

Year $7$ has $154$ pupils, and year $8$ has $110$ pupils.

What is the largest number of pupils which could be in each class?

**Question**

A school teaches French and German. All $165$ pupils in year $9$ study at least one language. 
Some pupils study just French, others study just German, and some study both French and German.

In total $112$ pupils study French, and $92$ pupils study German. How many pupils study only one language?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$1, \quad 2, \quad 4, \quad 8, \quad 16, \quad \ldots$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jane is making a spiral out of wire. She bends the wire 
after $1 \ \text{cm}$, then bends the wire 
after $2 \ \text{cm}$, then $3 \ \text{cm}$ and continues in this manner.
 
After $4$ bends she has used  $15 \ \text{cm}$ of wire.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2011/group-2/s01-q31-i00.png")

Complete the table

|Number of Bends         |$1$|$2$|$3$|$4$ |
|:----------------------:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:--:|
|Centimetres of wire used|   |   |   |$15$|


How much wire will she have used after $6$ bends?
 
How many bends will Jane have made if she uses $66 \ \text{cm}$ of wire?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jane is making a spiral out of wire. She bends the wire 
after $1 \ \text{cm}$, then bends the wire 
after $2 \ \text{cm}$, then $3 \ \text{cm}$ and continues in this manner.
<emptyline>
After $4$ bends she has used  $15 \ \text{cm}$ of wire.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Complete the table
<emptyline>
|Number of Bends         |$1$|$2$|$3$|$4$ |
|:----------------------:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:--:|
|Centimetres of wire used|   |   |   |$15$|
<emptyline>

How much wire will she have used after $6$ bends?
<emptyline>
How many bends will Jane have made if she uses $66 \ \text{cm}$ of wire?
</code></pre>
</div>
